Domain: `-1 < = x < = 1` Range: `0 < = y < = Pi` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_acosf for single-precision floats. The definition of `y = atan(x)` is `x = tan(y)`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-Pi/2 < = y < = Pi/2` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_atanf for single-precision floats. To calculate the arc tangent of y / x, use SDL_atan2. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atanf @sa SDL_atan2 @sa SDL_tan<br>返回：arc tangent of of `x` in radians, or 0 if `x = 0`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-1f8bbae43365bf17"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Atan2(double y, double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3028) | Compute the arc tangent of `y / x`, using the signs of x and y to adjust the result's quadrant. The definition of `z = atan2(x, y)` is `y = x tan(z)`, where the quadrant of z is determined based on the signs of x and y. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-Pi < = z < = Pi` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_atan2f for single-precision floats. To calculate the arc tangent of a single value, use SDL_atan. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atan2f @sa SDL_atan @sa SDL_tan<br>返回：arc tangent of `y / x` in radians, or, if `x = 0`, either `-Pi/2`, `0`, or `Pi/2`, depending on the value of `y`.<br>`y`：floating point value of the numerator (y coordinate).<br>`x`：floating point value of the denominator (x coordinate). |

| <a id="api-bd5ec38df33e8814"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.AudioDevicePaused(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L210) | Use this function to query if an audio device is paused. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. Physical and invalid device IDs will report themselves as unpaused here. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PauseAudioDevice @sa SDL_ResumeAudioDevice<br>返回：true if device is valid and paused, false otherwise.<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). |
| <a id="api-458a03eacb0eaa8d"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.CalculateGpuTextureFormatSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format, uint width, uint height, uint depth_or_layer_count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3707) | Calculate the size in bytes of a texture format with dimensions. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the size of a texture with this format and dimensions.<br>`format`：a texture format.<br>`width`：width in pixels.<br>`height`：height in pixels.<br>`depth_or_layer_count`：depth for 3D textures or layer count otherwise. |
| <a id="api-8114f2adbf008911"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.CaptureMouse(bool enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4183) | Capture the mouse and to track input outside an SDL window. Capturing enables your app to obtain mouse events globally, instead of just within your window. Not all video targets support this function. When capturing is enabled, the current window will get all mouse events, but unlike relative mode, no change is made to the cursor and it is not restrained to your window. This function may also deny mouse input to other windows--both those in your application and others on the system--so you should use this function sparingly, and in small bursts. For example, you might want to track the mouse while the user is dragging something, until the user releases a mouse button. It is not recommended that you capture the mouse for long periods of time, such as the entire time your app is running. For that, you should probably use SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode() or SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab(), depending on your goals. While captured, mouse events still report coordinates relative to the current (foreground) window, but those coordinates may be outside the bounds of the window (including negative values). Capturing is only allowed for the foreground window. If the window loses focus while capturing, the capture will be disabled automatically. While capturing is enabled, the current window will have the `SDL_WINDOW_MOUSE_CAPTURE` flag set. Please note that SDL will attempt to "auto capture" the mouse while the user is pressing a button; this is to try and make mouse behavior more consistent between platforms, and deal with the common case of a user dragging the mouse outside of the window. This means that if you are calling SDL_CaptureMouse() only to deal with this situation, you do not have to (although it is safe to do so). If this causes problems for your app, you can disable auto capture by setting the `SDL_HINT_MOUSE_AUTO_CAPTURE` hint to zero. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGlobalMouseState<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`enabled`：true to enable capturing, false to disable. |
| <a id="api-ed7c29187517526f"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Ceil(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2734) | Compute the ceiling of `x`. The ceiling of `x` is the smallest integer `y` such that `y >= x`, i.e `x` rounded up to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_ceilf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>返回：the ceiling of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-963eb74d0f44d92e"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Ceilf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3426) | Compute the ceiling of `x`. The ceiling of `x` is the smallest integer `y` such that `y >= x`, i.e `x` rounded up to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_ceil for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the ceiling of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |

| <a id="api-3bf0f09ca662fd47"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.CloseAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2238) | Close a previously-opened audio device. The application should close open audio devices once they are no longer needed. This function may block briefly while pending audio data is played by the hardware, so that applications don't drop the last buffer of data they supplied if terminating immediately afterwards. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_OpenAudioDevice<br>`devid`：an audio device id previously returned by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). |
| <a id="api-ffd7a7afc0082666"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ComposeCustomBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor srcColorFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor dstColorFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendOperation colorOperation, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor srcAlphaFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor dstAlphaFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendOperation alphaOperation)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1588) | Compose a custom blend mode for renderers. The functions SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode and SDL_SetTextureBlendMode accept the SDL_BlendMode returned by this function if the renderer supports it. A blend mode controls how the pixels from a drawing operation (source) get combined with the pixels from the render target (destination). First, the components of the source and destination pixels get multiplied with their blend factors. Then, the blend operation takes the two products and calculates the result that will get stored in the render target. Expressed in pseudocode, it would look like this: ```c dstRGB = colorOperation(srcRGB * srcColorFactor, dstRGB * dstColorFactor); dstA = alphaOperation(srcA * srcAlphaFactor, dstA * dstAlphaFactor); ``` Where the functions `colorOperation(src, dst)` and `alphaOperation(src, dst)` can return one of the following: - `src + dst` - `src - dst` - `dst - src` - `min(src, dst)` - `max(src, dst)` The red, green, and blue components are always multiplied with the first, second, and third components of the SDL_BlendFactor, respectively. The fourth component is not used. The alpha component is always multiplied with the fourth component of the SDL_BlendFactor. The other components are not used in the alpha calculation. Support for these blend modes varies for each renderer. To check if a specific SDL_BlendMode is supported, create a renderer and pass it to either SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode or SDL_SetTextureBlendMode. They will return with an error if the blend mode is not supported. This list describes the support of custom blend modes for each renderer. All renderers support the four blend modes listed in the SDL_BlendMode enumeration. - **direct3d**: Supports all operations with all factors. However, some factors produce unexpected results with `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_MINIMUM` and `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_MAXIMUM`. - **direct3d11**: Same as Direct3D 9. - **opengl**: Supports the `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_ADD` operation with all factors. OpenGL versions 1.1, 1.2, and 1.3 do not work correctly here. - **opengles2**: Supports the `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_ADD`, `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_SUBTRACT`, `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_REV_SUBTRACT` operations with all factors. - **psp**: No custom blend mode support. - **software**: No custom blend mode support. Some renderers do not provide an alpha component for the default render target. The `SDL_BLENDFACTOR_DST_ALPHA` and `SDL_BLENDFACTOR_ONE_MINUS_DST_ALPHA` factors do not have an effect in this case. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawBlendMode @sa SDL_SetTextureBlendMode @sa SDL_GetTextureBlendMode<br>返回：an SDL_BlendMode that represents the chosen factors and operations.<br>`srcColorFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the red, green, and blue components of the source pixels.<br>`dstColorFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the red, green, and blue components of the destination pixels.<br>`colorOperation`：the SDL_BlendOperation used to combine the red, green, and blue components of the source and destination pixels.<br>`srcAlphaFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the alpha component of the source pixels.<br>`dstAlphaFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the alpha component of the destination pixels.<br>`alphaOperation`：the SDL_BlendOperation used to combine the alpha component of the source and destination pixels. |
| <a id="api-b93a5f2c1215fa95"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.CopyProperties(uint src, uint dst)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L501) | Copy a group of properties. Copy all the properties from one group of properties to another, with the exception of properties requiring cleanup (set using SDL_SetPointerPropertyWithCleanup()), which will not be copied. Any property that already exists on `dst` will be overwritten. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. This function acquires simultaneous mutex locks on both the source and destination property sets. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`src`：the properties to copy.<br>`dst`：the destination properties. |
| <a id="api-2faab86a6b048fc4"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Copysign(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1044) | Copy the sign of one floating-point value to another. The definition of copysign is that ``copysign(x, y) = abs(x) * sign(y)``. | <a id="api-6373f45f5e689f9b"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Exp(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L71) | Compute the exponential of `x`. The definition of `y = exp(x)` is `y = e^x`, where `e` is the base of the natural logarithm. The inverse is the natural logarithm, SDL_log. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` The output will overflow if `exp(x)` is too large to be represented. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_expf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_expf @sa SDL_log<br>返回：value of `e^x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-e02aea49f7649903"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Expf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2777) | Compute the exponential of `x`. The definition of `y = exp(x)` is `y = e^x`, where `e` is the base of the natural logarithm. The inverse is the natural logarithm, SDL_logf. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` The output will overflow if `exp(x)` is too large to be represented. This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_exp for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_exp @sa SDL_logf<br>返回：value of `e^x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |

The floor of `x` is the largest integer `y` such that `y < = x`, i.e `x` rounded down to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_floorf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>返回：the floor of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-6236831d124430a0"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Floorf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3687) | Compute the floor of `x`. The floor of `x` is the largest integer `y` such that `y < = x`, i.e `x` rounded down to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_floor for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the floor of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-b97d4b3cf53bb2d3"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.FlushEvent(uint type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4317) | Clear events of a specific type from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that match `type`. If you need to remove a range of event types, use SDL_FlushEvents() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. If you have user events with custom data that needs to be freed, you should use SDL_PeepEvents() to remove and clean up those events before calling this function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvents<br>`type`：the type of event to be cleared; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-a7203ea9b797cf45"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.FlushEvents(uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3883) | Clear events of a range of types from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that are in the range of `minType` to `maxType`, inclusive. If you need to remove a single event type, use SDL_FlushEvent() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvent<br>`minType`：the low end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`maxType`：the high end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-63c922dd60d46477"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Fmod(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2383) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmodf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmodf @sa SDL_modf @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`.<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0. |
| <a id="api-804e2a771efcef9e"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Fmodf(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1244) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmod for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmod @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_modff @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`.<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0. |

If gamepad events are disabled, you must call SDL_UpdateGamepads() yourself and check the state of the gamepad when you want gamepad information. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetGamepadEventsEnabled<br>返回：true if gamepad events are being processed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-0276de1d14750fb2"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetAudioDeviceGain(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L673) | Get the gain of an audio device. The gain of a device is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio devices default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). Physical devices may not have their gain changed, only logical devices, and this function will always return -1.0f when used on physical devices. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioDeviceGain<br>返回：the gain of the device or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`devid`：the audio device to query. |
| <a id="api-8dd143ba2e72e810"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraPosition Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetCameraPosition(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L690) | Get the position of the camera in relation to the system. Most platforms will report UNKNOWN, but mobile devices, like phones, can often make a distinction between cameras on the front of the device (that points towards the user, for taking "selfies") and cameras on the back (for filming in the direction the user is facing). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameras<br>返回：the position of the camera on the system hardware.<br>`instance_id`：the camera device instance ID. |
| <a id="api-5736f0a1841ee7b6"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetCpuCacheLineSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4363) | Determine the L1 cache line size of the CPU. This is useful for determining multi-threaded structure padding or SIMD prefetch sizes. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the L1 cache line size of the CPU, in bytes. |
| <a id="api-1c5503a5002ea1bf"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetCurrentDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2032) | Get the orientation of a display. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-8fa8cfadccc79fdb"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetCurrentThreadId()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2985) | Get the thread identifier for the current thread. This thread identifier is as reported by the underlying operating system. If SDL is running on a platform that does not support threads the return value will always be zero. This function also returns a valid thread ID when called from the main thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetThreadID<br>返回：the ID of the current thread. |
| <a id="api-218dd9d09d704359"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDayOfWeek(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1063) | Get the day of week for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a value between 0 and 6 (0 being Sunday) if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date. |
| <a id="api-8e1218528765be00"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDayOfYear(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3086) | Get the day of year for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the day of year [0-365] if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date. |
| <a id="api-1092c4c33439191f"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDaysInMonth(int year, int month)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L271) | Get the number of days in a month for a given year. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the number of days in the requested month or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year.<br>`month`：the month [1-12]. |
| <a id="api-440389b2acd90d0e"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDirect3D9AdapterIndex(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3145) | Get the D3D9 adapter index that matches the specified display. The returned adapter index can be passed to `IDirect3D9::CreateDevice` and controls on which monitor a full screen application will appear. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the D3D9 adapter index on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-f02d7b5e23c3a8ba"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDisplayContentScale(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1502) | Get the content scale of a display. The content scale is the expected scale for content based on the DPI settings of the display. For example, a 4K display might have a 2.0 (200%) display scale, which means that the user expects UI elements to be twice as big on this display, to aid in readability. After window creation, SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale() should be used to query the content scale factor for individual windows instead of querying the display for a window and calling this function, as the per-window content scale factor may differ from the base value of the display it is on, particularly on high-DPI and/or multi-monitor desktop configurations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the content scale of the display, or 0.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-f00d59bdf8408474"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetDisplayProperties(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1848) | Get the properties associated with a display. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_HDR_ENABLED_BOOLEAN`: true if the display has HDR headroom above the SDR white point. This is for informational and diagnostic purposes only, as not all platforms provide this information at the display level. On KMS/DRM: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_KMSDRM_PANEL_ORIENTATION_NUMBER`: the "panel orientation" property for the display in degrees of clockwise rotation. Note that this is provided only as a hint, and the application is responsible for any coordinate transformations needed to conform to the requested display orientation. On Wayland: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WAYLAND_WL_OUTPUT_POINTER`: the wl_output associated with the display On Windows: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WINDOWS_HMONITOR_POINTER`: the monitor handle (HMONITOR) associated with the display @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |

If you want to get the keycode as it would be delivered in key events, including options specified in SDL_HINT_KEYCODE_OPTIONS, then you should pass `key_event` as true. Otherwise this function simply translates the scancode based on the given modifier state. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyName @sa SDL_GetScancodeFromKey<br>返回：the SDL_Keycode that corresponds to the given SDL_Scancode.<br>`scancode`：the desired SDL_Scancode to query.<br>`modstate`：the modifier state to use when translating the scancode to a keycode.<br>`key_event`：true if the keycode will be used in key events. |

| <a id="api-3b9914316393d7f4"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNaturalDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2461) | Get the orientation of a display when it is unrotated. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |

| <a id="api-3f3bbf0ab4d87b32"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNumAudioDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3119) | Use this function to get the number of built-in audio drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have esound support, but if there's no esound server available, SDL's esound driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in audio drivers. |
| <a id="api-fd272d41c64dfefb"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNumCameraDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2948) | Use this function to get the number of built-in camera drivers. This function returns a hardcoded number. This never returns a negative value; if there are no drivers compiled into this build of SDL, this function returns zero. The presence of a driver in this list does not mean it will function, it just means SDL is capable of interacting with that interface. For example, a build of SDL might have v4l2 support, but if there's no kernel support available, SDL's v4l2 driver would fail if used. By default, SDL tries all drivers, in its preferred order, until one is found to be usable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameraDriver<br>返回：the number of built-in camera drivers. |
| <a id="api-595f5bc4491544d4"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNumGpuDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1356) | Get the number of GPU drivers compiled into SDL. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGPUDriver<br>返回：the number of built in GPU drivers. |
| <a id="api-cb5f400a1862e551"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNumLogicalCpuCores()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1009) | Get the number of logical CPU cores available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the total number of logical CPU cores. On CPUs that include technologies such as hyperthreading, the number of logical cores may be more than the number of physical cores. |
| <a id="api-137e8f0cfed3f8c1"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetNumRenderDrivers()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3441) | Get the number of 2D rendering drivers available for the current display. A render driver is a set of code that handles rendering and texture management on a particular display. Normally there is only one, but some drivers may have several available with different capabilities. There may be none if SDL was compiled without render support. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateRenderer @sa SDL_GetRenderDriver<br>返回：the number of built in render drivers. |

Many platforms do not supply this information, so an app must always be prepared to get an SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_UNKNOWN result. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>返回：the device type of the given pen, or SDL_PEN_DEVICE_TYPE_INVALID on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`instance_id`：the pen instance ID. |
| <a id="api-c27d241830e5f7c2"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetPerformanceCounter()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1347) | Get the current value of the high resolution counter. This function is typically used for profiling. The counter values are only meaningful relative to each other. Differences between values can be converted to times by using SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceFrequency<br>返回：the current counter value. |
| <a id="api-2e375e480d0ab7f2"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetPerformanceFrequency()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2744) | Get the count per second of the high resolution counter. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPerformanceCounter<br>返回：a platform-specific count per second. |
| <a id="api-756745e8740bc30b"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetPixelFormatForMasks(int bpp, uint Rmask, uint Gmask, uint Bmask, uint Amask)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1818) | Convert a bpp value and RGBA masks to an enumerated pixel format. This will return `SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN` if the conversion wasn't possible. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMasksForPixelFormat<br>返回：the SDL_PixelFormat value corresponding to the format masks, or SDL_PIXELFORMAT_UNKNOWN if there isn't a match.<br>`bpp`：a bits per pixel value; usually 15, 16, or 32.<br>`Rmask`：the red mask for the format.<br>`Gmask`：the green mask for the format.<br>`Bmask`：the blue mask for the format.<br>`Amask`：the alpha mask for the format. |

| <a id="api-c87a38aeef37860c"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSensorNonPortableTypeForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1124) | Get the platform dependent type of a sensor. This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the sensor platform dependent type, or -1 if `instance_id` is not valid.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID. |

This can be called before any sensors are opened. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the SDL_SensorType, or `SDL_SENSOR_INVALID` if `instance_id` is not valid.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID. |
| <a id="api-72985eff517dfa91"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSilenceValueForFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioFormat format)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2917) | Get the appropriate memset value for silencing an audio format. The value returned by this function can be used as the second argument to memset (or SDL_memset) to set an audio buffer in a specific format to silence. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a byte value that can be passed to memset.<br>`format`：the audio data format to query. |
| <a id="api-0dffe258eacc5c3c"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSimdAlignment()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3987) | Report the alignment this system needs for SIMD allocations. This will return the minimum number of bytes to which a pointer must be aligned to be compatible with SIMD instructions on the current machine. For example, if the machine supports SSE only, it will return 16, but if it supports AVX-512F, it'll return 64 (etc). This only reports values for instruction sets SDL knows about, so if your SDL build doesn't have SDL_HasAVX512F(), then it might return 16 for the SSE support it sees and not 64 for the AVX-512 instructions that exist but SDL doesn't know about. Plan accordingly. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_aligned_alloc @sa SDL_aligned_free<br>返回：the alignment in bytes needed for available, known SIMD instructions. |
| <a id="api-583f910a4497fd42"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSystemPageSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2018) | Report the size of a page of memory. Different platforms might have different memory page sizes. In current times, 4 kilobytes is not unusual, but newer systems are moving to larger page sizes, and esoteric platforms might have any unexpected size. Note that this function can return 0, which means SDL can't determine the page size on this platform. It will _not_ set an error string to be retrieved with SDL_GetError() in this case! In this case, defaulting to 4096 is often a reasonable option. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>返回：the size of a single page of memory, in bytes, or 0 if SDL can't determine this information. |
| <a id="api-70fee50bd08efd12"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSystemRam()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4086) | Get the amount of RAM configured in the system. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the amount of RAM configured in the system in MiB. |
| <a id="api-de79c0736e692910"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSystemTheme Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetSystemTheme()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3067) | Get the current system theme. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the current system theme, light, dark, or unknown. |
| <a id="api-2113f250694a267d"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetTicks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L479) | Get the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTicksNS<br>返回：an unsigned 64‑bit integer that represents the number of milliseconds that have elapsed since the SDL library was initialized (typically via a call to SDL_Init). |
| <a id="api-230fcee698d8fe75"></a>[`public static ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetTicksNs()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2869) | Get the number of nanoseconds since SDL library initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：an unsigned 64-bit value representing the number of nanoseconds since the SDL library initialized. |

| <a id="api-438c97113b89a00d"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GetVersion()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2512) | Get the version of SDL that is linked against your program. If you are linking to SDL dynamically, then it is possible that the current version will be different than the version you compiled against. This function returns the current version, while SDL_VERSION is the version you compiled with. This function may be called safely at any time, even before SDL_Init(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRevision<br>返回：the version of the linked library. |
| <a id="api-f9d7ebd3ce04555d"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GlResetAttributes()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3238) | Reset all previously set OpenGL context attributes to their default values. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_SetAttribute |
| <a id="api-9ca6472d80755c8e"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GlSetAttribute(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGLAttr attr, int value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1899) | Set an OpenGL window attribute before window creation. This function sets the OpenGL attribute `attr` to `value`. The requested attributes should be set before creating an OpenGL window. You should use SDL_GL_GetAttribute() to check the values after creating the OpenGL context, since the values obtained can differ from the requested ones. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_CreateContext @sa SDL_GL_GetAttribute @sa SDL_GL_ResetAttributes<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`attr`：an enum value specifying the OpenGL attribute to set.<br>`value`：the desired value for the attribute. |
| <a id="api-85c55f55bfd572e6"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.GlSetSwapInterval(int interval)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L298) | Set the swap interval for the current OpenGL context. Some systems allow specifying -1 for the interval, to enable adaptive vsync. Adaptive vsync works the same as vsync, but if you've already missed the vertical retrace for a given frame, it swaps buffers immediately, which might be less jarring for the user during occasional framerate drops. If an application requests adaptive vsync and the system does not support it, this function will fail and return false. In such a case, you should probably retry the call with 1 for the interval. Adaptive vsync is implemented for some glX drivers with GLX_EXT_swap_control_tear, and for some Windows drivers with WGL_EXT_swap_control_tear. Read more on the Khronos wiki: https://www.khronos.org/opengl/wiki/Swap_Interval#Adaptive_Vsync @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GL_GetSwapInterval<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`interval`：0 for immediate updates, 1 for updates synchronized with the vertical retrace, -1 for adaptive vsync. |

| <a id="api-5b0a4e05221c08dc"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.HidDeviceChangeCount()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3900) | Check to see if devices may have been added or removed. Enumerating the HID devices is an expensive operation, so you can call this to see if there have been any system device changes since the last call to this function. A change in the counter returned doesn't necessarily mean that anything has changed, but you can call SDL_hid_enumerate() to get an updated device list. Calling this function for the first time may cause a thread or other system resource to be allocated to track device change notifications. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_enumerate<br>返回：a change counter that is incremented with each potential device change, or 0 if device change detection isn't available. |
| <a id="api-9d27cb4b2b976dc8"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.HidExit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3999) | Finalize the HIDAPI library. This function frees all of the static data associated with HIDAPI. It should be called at the end of execution to avoid memory leaks. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_init<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7eacf14df81cdcf6"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.HidInit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4227) | Initialize the HIDAPI library. This function initializes the HIDAPI library. Calling it is not strictly necessary, as it will be called automatically by SDL_hid_enumerate(), SDL_hid_open(), and SDL_hid_open_path() if needed. This function should be called at the beginning of execution however, if there is a chance of HIDAPI handles being opened by different threads simultaneously. Each call to this function should have a matching call to SDL_hid_exit() @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_hid_exit<br>返回：0 on success or a negative error code on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c40ac414dfc91946"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.HideCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2044) | Hide the cursor. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CursorVisible @sa SDL_ShowCursor<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0ca657728fd006e6"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Init(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitFlags flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3332) | Initialize the SDL library. SDL_Init() simply forwards to calling SDL_InitSubSystem(). Therefore, the two may be used interchangeably. Though for readability of your code SDL_InitSubSystem() might be preferred. The file I/O (for example: SDL_IOFromFile) and threading (SDL_CreateThread) subsystems are initialized by default. Message boxes (SDL_ShowSimpleMessageBox) also attempt to work without initializing the video subsystem, in hopes of being useful in showing an error dialog when SDL_Init fails. You must specifically initialize other subsystems if you use them in your application. Logging (such as SDL_Log) works without initialization, too. `flags` may be any of the following OR'd together: - `SDL_INIT_AUDIO`: audio subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_VIDEO`: video subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem, should be initialized on the main thread. - `SDL_INIT_JOYSTICK`: joystick subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_HAPTIC`: haptic (force feedback) subsystem - `SDL_INIT_GAMEPAD`: gamepad subsystem; automatically initializes the joystick subsystem - `SDL_INIT_EVENTS`: events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_SENSOR`: sensor subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem - `SDL_INIT_CAMERA`: camera subsystem; automatically initializes the events subsystem Subsystem initialization is ref-counted, you must call SDL_QuitSubSystem() for each SDL_InitSubSystem() to correctly shutdown a subsystem manually (or call SDL_Quit() to force shutdown). If a subsystem is already loaded then this call will increase the ref-count and return. Consider reporting some basic metadata about your application before calling SDL_Init, using either SDL_SetAppMetadata() or SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAppMetadata @sa SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty @sa SDL_InitSubSystem @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_SetMainReady @sa SDL_WasInit<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`flags`：subsystem initialization flags. |
| <a id="api-0cd8b537ee0189cc"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.InitSubSystem(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitFlags flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3379) | Compatibility function to initialize the SDL library. This function and SDL_Init() are interchangeable. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_Quit @sa SDL_QuitSubSystem<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details. |
| <a id="api-68f0f5b1fafd9fa3"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.IsAudioDevicePhysical(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2418) | Determine if an audio device is physical (instead of logical). An SDL_AudioDeviceID that represents physical hardware is a physical device; there is one for each piece of hardware that SDL can see. Logical devices are created by calling SDL_OpenAudioDevice or SDL_OpenAudioDeviceStream, and while each is associated with a physical device, there can be any number of logical devices on one physical device. For the most part, logical and physical IDs are interchangeable--if you try to open a logical device, SDL understands to assign that effort to the underlying physical device, etc. However, it might be useful to know if an arbitrary device ID is physical or logical. This function reports which. This function may return either true or false for invalid device IDs. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true if devid is a physical device, false if it is logical.<br>`devid`：the device ID to query. |

On Apple platforms, the main thread is the thread that runs your program's main() entry point. Obtain a multi-threaded lock for these properties. Other threads will wait while trying to lock these properties until they are unlocked. Properties must be unlocked before they are destroyed. The lock is automatically taken when setting individual properties, this function is only needed when you want to set several properties atomically or want to guarantee that properties being queried aren't freed in another thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UnlockProperties<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`props`：the properties to lock. |

| <a id="api-5d1003fb2d95c744"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.MemoryBarrierAcquireFunction()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2963) | Insert a memory acquire barrier (function version). Please refer to SDL_MemoryBarrierRelease for details. This is a function version, which might be useful if you need to use this functionality from a scripting language, etc. Also, some of the macro versions call this function behind the scenes, where more heavy lifting can happen inside of SDL. Generally, though, an app written in C/C++/etc should use the macro version, as it will be more efficient. @threadsafety Obviously this function is safe to use from any thread at any time, but if you find yourself needing this, you are probably dealing with some very sensitive code; be careful! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_MemoryBarrierAcquire |

This functions allows iOS apps that have their own event handling to hook into SDL to generate SDL events. This maps directly to an iOS-specific event, but since it doesn't do anything iOS-specific internally, it is available on all platforms, in case it might be useful for some specific paradigm. Most apps do not need to use this directly; SDL's internal event code will handle all this for windows created by SDL_CreateWindow! @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-75d002bc156242aa"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.OutOfMemory()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3193) | Set an error indicating that memory allocation failed. This function does not do any memory allocation. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：false. |
| <a id="api-ec2f24b2fd2c7790"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.PauseAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2264) | Use this function to pause audio playback on a specified device. This function pauses audio processing for a given device. Any bound audio streams will not progress, and no audio will be generated. Pausing one device does not prevent other unpaused devices from running. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Pausing a paused device is a legal no-op. Pausing a device can be useful to halt all audio without unbinding all the audio streams. This might be useful while a game is paused, or a level is loading, etc. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResumeAudioDevice @sa SDL_AudioDevicePaused<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). |
| <a id="api-edee07c22fb287ba"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Pow(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2859) | Raise `x` to the power `y` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-INF < = z < = INF` If `y` is the base of the natural logarithm (e), consider using SDL_exp instead. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_powf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_powf @sa SDL_exp @sa SDL_log<br>返回：`x` raised to the power `y`.<br>`x`：the base.<br>`y`：the exponent. |
| <a id="api-ce427351dabda45f"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Powf(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1468) | Raise `x` to the power `y` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-INF < = z < = INF` If `y` is the base of the natural logarithm (e), consider using SDL_exp instead. This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_pow for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_pow @sa SDL_expf @sa SDL_logf<br>返回：`x` raised to the power `y`.<br>`x`：the base.<br>`y`：the exponent. |
| <a id="api-d640f7a3df37b430"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.PumpEvents()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2688) | Pump the event loop, gathering events from the input devices. This function updates the event queue and internal input device state. SDL_PumpEvents() gathers all the pending input information from devices and places it in the event queue. Without calls to SDL_PumpEvents() no events would ever be placed on the queue. Often the need for calls to SDL_PumpEvents() is hidden from the user since SDL_PollEvent() and SDL_WaitEvent() implicitly call SDL_PumpEvents(). However, if you are not polling or waiting for events (e.g. you are filtering them), then you must call SDL_PumpEvents() to force an event queue update. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_WaitEvent |
| <a id="api-87291d947d9a3f98"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Quit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1876) | Clean up all initialized subsystems. You should call this function even if you have already shutdown each initialized subsystem with SDL_QuitSubSystem(). It is safe to call this function even in the case of errors in initialization. You can use this function with atexit() to ensure that it is run when your application is shutdown, but it is not wise to do this from a library or other dynamically loaded code. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_QuitSubSystem |
| <a id="api-1cf871cfc14de885"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.QuitSubSystem(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitFlags flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1257) | Shut down specific SDL subsystems. You still need to call SDL_Quit() even if you close all open subsystems with SDL_QuitSubSystem(). @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InitSubSystem @sa SDL_Quit<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details. |
| <a id="api-a993105bc85e6ddf"></a>[`public static int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Rand(int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3735) | Generate a pseudo-random number less than n for positive n The method used is faster and of better quality than `rand() % n`. Odds are roughly 99.9% even for n = 1 million. Evenness is better for smaller n, and much worse as n gets bigger. Example: to simulate a d6 use `SDL_rand(6) + 1` The +1 converts 0..5 to 1..6 If you want to generate a pseudo-random number in the full range of Sint32, you should use: (Sint32)SDL_rand_bits() If you want reproducible output, be sure to initialize with SDL_srand() first. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_srand @sa SDL_randf<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0 .. n-1].<br>`n`：the number of possible outcomes. n must be positive. |
| <a id="api-dd45dd1d2ea4e641"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.RandBits()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4017) | Generate 32 pseudo-random bits. You likely want to use SDL_rand() to get a pseudo-random number instead. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand @sa SDL_randf @sa SDL_srand<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0-SDL_MAX_UINT32]. |
| <a id="api-26b57b8337cb2688"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Randf()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L4141) | Generate a uniform pseudo-random floating point number less than 1.0 If you want reproducible output, be sure to initialize with SDL_srand() first. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_srand @sa SDL_rand<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0.0, 1.0). |
| <a id="api-548117b7953634a1"></a>[`public static uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.RegisterEvents(int numevents)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1630) | Allocate a set of user-defined events, and return the beginning event number for that set of events. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PushEvent<br>返回：the beginning event number, or 0 if numevents is invalid or if there are not enough user-defined events left.<br>`numevents`：the number of events to be allocated. |

This will generate gamepad events as needed if device mappings change. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b308f40f5dd5a2fd"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.RemoveTimer(uint id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2278) | Remove a timer created with SDL_AddTimer(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddTimer<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`id`：the ID of the timer to remove. |
| <a id="api-f7d68d1b6c3dacf5"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ResetAssertionReport()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2930) | Clear the list of all assertion failures. This function will clear the list of all assertions triggered up to that point. Immediately following this call, SDL_GetAssertionReport will return no items. In addition, any previously-triggered assertions will be reset to a trigger_count of zero, and their always_ignore state will be false. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. Other threads triggering an assertion, or simultaneously calling this function may cause memory leaks or crashes. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAssertionReport |
| <a id="api-91aea35ba8556c1c"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ResetHints()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2573) | Reset all hints to the default values. This will reset all hints to the value of the associated environment variable, or NULL if the environment isn't set. Callbacks will be called normally with this change. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResetHint |
| <a id="api-1db27a4efd2a11dd"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ResetKeyboard()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2103) | Clear the state of the keyboard. | <a id="api-7afd4cdc472044a3"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ResetLogPriorities()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1759) | Reset all priorities to default. This is called by SDL_Quit(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogPriorities @sa SDL_SetLogPriority |
| <a id="api-6fce7f646ef78e35"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ResumeAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L1281) | Use this function to unpause audio playback on a specified device. This function unpauses audio processing for a given device that has previously been paused with SDL_PauseAudioDevice(). Once unpaused, any bound audio streams will begin to progress again, and audio can be generated. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Unpausing an unpaused device is a legal no-op. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AudioDevicePaused @sa SDL_PauseAudioDevice<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). |
| <a id="api-12dbcfe01954d86b"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Round(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2147) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_roundf for single-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lround. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lround @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_trunc<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-245ae8c3e61391a9"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Roundf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L255) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_round for double-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lroundf. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lroundf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-5d7ac40b0ba7bacb"></a>[`public static double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Scalbn(double x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3654) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbnf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbnf @sa SDL_pow<br>返回：`x * 2^n`.<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent. |
| <a id="api-ae276a58fc8fcf07"></a>[`public static float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.Scalbnf(float x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2563) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbn for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbn @sa SDL_powf<br>返回：`x * 2^n`.<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent. |
| <a id="api-1c081d999633a52e"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.ScreenSaverEnabled()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L3158) | Check whether the screensaver is currently enabled. The screensaver is disabled by default. The default can also be changed using `SDL_HINT_VIDEO_ALLOW_SCREENSAVER`. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DisableScreenSaver @sa SDL_EnableScreenSaver<br>返回：true if the screensaver is enabled, false if it is disabled. |
| <a id="api-94f87c8292008fbd"></a>[`public static bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.SetAudioDeviceGain(uint devid, float gain)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2310) | Change the gain of an audio device. The gain of a device is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio devices default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). Physical devices may not have their gain changed, only logical devices, and this function will always return false when used on physical devices. While it might seem attractive to adjust several logical devices at once in this way, it would allow an app or library to interfere with another portion of the program's otherwise-isolated devices. This is applied, along with any per-audiostream gain, during playback to the hardware, and can be continuously changed to create various effects. On recording devices, this will adjust the gain before passing the data into an audiostream; that recording audiostream can then adjust its gain further when outputting the data elsewhere, if it likes, but that second gain is not applied until the data leaves the audiostream again. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioDeviceGain<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`devid`：the audio device on which to change gain.<br>`gain`：the gain. 1.0f is no change, 0.0f is silence. |

| <a id="api-dc2b430874a3bd97"></a>[`public static void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDL.UpdateTrays()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L2533) | Update the trays. | <a id="api-02d9a1aa69580842"></a>[`public virtual void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioPostmixCallback.Invoke(void* argument0, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* argument1, float* argument2, int argument3)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L53939) | A callback that fires when data is about to be fed to an audio device. This is useful for accessing the final mix, perhaps for writing a visualizer or applying a final effect to the audio data before playback. This callback should run as quickly as possible and not block for any significant time, as this callback delays submission of data to the audio device, which can cause audio playback problems. The postmix callback _must_ be able to handle any audio data format specified in `spec`, which can change between callbacks if the audio device changed. However, this only covers frequency and channel count; data is always provided here in SDL_AUDIO_F32 format. The postmix callback runs _after_ logical device gain and audiostream gain have been applied, which is to say you can make the output data louder at this point than the gain settings would suggest. @param userdata a pointer provided by the app through SDL_SetAudioPostmixCallback, for its own use. @param spec the current format of audio that is to be submitted to the audio device. @param buffer the buffer of audio samples to be submitted. The callback can inspect and/or modify this data. @param buflen the size of `buffer` in bytes. @threadsafety This will run from a background thread owned by SDL. The application is responsible for locking resources the callback touches that need to be protected. @since This datatype is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioPostmixCallback |

| <a id="api-4b0627245a8aafaf"></a>[`public virtual System.IAsyncResult Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStreamCallback.BeginInvoke(void* argument0, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* argument1, int argument2, int argument3, System.AsyncCallback callback, object @object)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L54035) | 当前生成的 ABI 声明；地址、缓冲区及回调的有效期由所属原生 owner 保证。 |

| <a id="api-9f1e7740a851e473"></a>[`public virtual void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStreamCallback.Invoke(void* argument0, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* argument1, int argument2, int argument3)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L54035) | A callback that fires when data passes through an SDL_AudioStream. Apps can (optionally) register a callback with an audio stream that is called when data is added with SDL_PutAudioStreamData, or requested with SDL_GetAudioStreamData. Two values are offered here: one is the amount of additional data needed to satisfy the immediate request (which might be zero if the stream already has enough data queued) and the other is the total amount being requested. In a Get call triggering a Put callback, these values can be different. In a Put call triggering a Get callback, these values are always the same. Byte counts might be slightly overestimated due to buffering or resampling, and may change from call to call. This callback is not required to do anything. Generally this is useful for adding/reading data on demand, and the app will often put/get data as appropriate, but the system goes on with the data currently available to it if this callback does nothing. @param stream the SDL audio stream associated with this callback. @param additional_amount the amount of data, in bytes, that is needed right now. @param total_amount the total amount of data requested, in bytes, that is requested or available. @param userdata an opaque pointer provided by the app for their personal use. @threadsafety This callbacks may run from any thread, so if you need to protect shared data, you should use SDL_LockAudioStream to serialize access; this lock will be held before your callback is called, so your callback does not need to manage the lock explicitly. @since This datatype is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamGetCallback @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamPutCallback |
| <a id="api-14d9b46bee6bd518"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStreamDataCompleteCallback.SDLAudioStreamDataCompleteCallback(object @object, nint method)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L54069) | 当前生成的 ABI 声明；地址、缓冲区及回调的有效期由所属原生 owner 保证。 |

| <a id="api-88d0815b9c8923be"></a>[`public virtual void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStreamDataCompleteCallback.Invoke(void* argument0, void* argument1, int argument2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L54069) | A callback that fires for completed SDL_PutAudioStreamDataNoCopy() data. When using SDL_PutAudioStreamDataNoCopy() to provide data to an SDL_AudioStream, it's not safe to dispose of the data until the stream has completely consumed it. Often times it's difficult to know exactly when this has happened. This callback fires once when the stream no longer needs the buffer, allowing the app to easily free or reuse it. @param userdata an opaque pointer provided by the app for their personal use. @param buf the pointer provided to SDL_PutAudioStreamDataNoCopy(). @param buflen the size of buffer, in bytes, provided to SDL_PutAudioStreamDataNoCopy(). @threadsafety This callbacks may run from any thread, so if you need to protect shared data, you should use SDL_LockAudioStream to serialize access; this lock will be held before your callback is called, so your callback does not need to manage the lock explicitly. @since This datatype is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamGetCallback @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamPutCallback |

The clipboard is automatically cleared in SDL_Quit(). @param userdata a pointer to the provided user data. @param mime_type the requested mime-type. @param size a pointer filled in with the length of the returned data. @returns a pointer to the data for the provided mime-type. Returning NULL or setting the length to 0 will cause zero length data to be sent to the "receiver", which should be able to handle this. The returned data will not be freed, so it needs to be retained and dealt with internally. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetClipboardData |

Domain: `-1 < = x < = 1` Range: `0 < = y < = Pi` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_acosf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_acosf @sa SDL_asin @sa SDL_cos<br>返回：arc cosine of `x`, in radians.<br>`x`：floating point value. |

| <a id="api-3c5a98153d27946f"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Atan(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14603) | Compute the arc tangent of `x`. The definition of `y = atan(x)` is `x = tan(y)`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-Pi/2 < = y < = Pi/2` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_atanf for single-precision floats. To calculate the arc tangent of y / x, use SDL_atan2. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atanf @sa SDL_atan2 @sa SDL_tan<br>返回：arc tangent of of `x` in radians, or 0 if `x = 0`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
