The definition of `z = atan2(x, y)` is `y = x tan(z)`, where the quadrant of z is determined based on the signs of x and y. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-Pi < = z < = Pi` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_atan2f for single-precision floats. To calculate the arc tangent of a single value, use SDL_atan. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atan2f @sa SDL_atan @sa SDL_tan<br>返回：arc tangent of `y / x` in radians, or, if `x = 0`, either `-Pi/2`, `0`, or `Pi/2`, depending on the value of `y`.<br>`y`：floating point value of the numerator (y coordinate).<br>`x`：floating point value of the denominator (x coordinate). |

To calculate the arc tangent of a single value, use SDL_atanf. | <a id="api-1bb92fdde9a70a56"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Atanf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16590) | Compute the arc tangent of `x`. The definition of `y = atan(x)` is `x = tan(y)`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-Pi/2 < = y < = Pi/2` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_atan for dboule-precision floats. To calculate the arc tangent of y / x, use SDL_atan2f. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atan @sa SDL_atan2f @sa SDL_tanf<br>返回：arc tangent of of `x` in radians, or 0 if `x = 0`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-8973b45afb67a2bd"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.AudioDevicePaused(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11610) | Use this function to query if an audio device is paused. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. Physical and invalid device IDs will report themselves as unpaused here. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PauseAudioDevice @sa SDL_ResumeAudioDevice<br>返回：true if device is valid and paused, false otherwise.<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice(). |
| <a id="api-99fd6d3f23ff5dba"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.CalculateGpuTextureFormatSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureFormat format, uint width, uint height, uint depth_or_layer_count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15862) | Calculate the size in bytes of a texture format with dimensions. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the size of a texture with this format and dimensions.<br>`format`：a texture format.<br>`width`：width in pixels.<br>`height`：height in pixels.<br>`depth_or_layer_count`：depth for 3D textures or layer count otherwise. |
| <a id="api-e3b83a79d62d9727"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.CaptureMouse(bool enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16451) | Capture the mouse and to track input outside an SDL window. Capturing enables your app to obtain mouse events globally, instead of just within your window. Not all video targets support this function. When capturing is enabled, the current window will get all mouse events, but unlike relative mode, no change is made to the cursor and it is not restrained to your window. This function may also deny mouse input to other windows--both those in your application and others on the system--so you should use this function sparingly, and in small bursts. For example, you might want to track the mouse while the user is dragging something, until the user releases a mouse button. It is not recommended that you capture the mouse for long periods of time, such as the entire time your app is running. For that, you should probably use SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode() or SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab(), depending on your goals. While captured, mouse events still report coordinates relative to the current (foreground) window, but those coordinates may be outside the bounds of the window (including negative values). Capturing is only allowed for the foreground window. If the window loses focus while capturing, the capture will be disabled automatically. While capturing is enabled, the current window will have the `SDL_WINDOW_MOUSE_CAPTURE` flag set. Please note that SDL will attempt to "auto capture" the mouse while the user is pressing a button; this is to try and make mouse behavior more consistent between platforms, and deal with the common case of a user dragging the mouse outside of the window. This means that if you are calling SDL_CaptureMouse() only to deal with this situation, you do not have to (although it is safe to do so). If this causes problems for your app, you can disable auto capture by setting the `SDL_HINT_MOUSE_AUTO_CAPTURE` hint to zero. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGlobalMouseState<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`enabled`：true to enable capturing, false to disable. |
| <a id="api-715b8474bd955639"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Ceil(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14677) | Compute the ceiling of `x`. The ceiling of `x` is the smallest integer `y` such that `y >= x`, i.e `x` rounded up to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_ceilf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>返回：the ceiling of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-b1eb60949f6c3f2f"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Ceilf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15521) | Compute the ceiling of `x`. The ceiling of `x` is the smallest integer `y` such that `y >= x`, i.e `x` rounded up to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_ceil for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the ceiling of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |

| <a id="api-668c28f5471661d9"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.ComposeCustomBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor srcColorFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor dstColorFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendOperation colorOperation, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor srcAlphaFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendFactor dstAlphaFactor, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLBlendOperation alphaOperation)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13289) | Compose a custom blend mode for renderers. The functions SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode and SDL_SetTextureBlendMode accept the SDL_BlendMode returned by this function if the renderer supports it. A blend mode controls how the pixels from a drawing operation (source) get combined with the pixels from the render target (destination). First, the components of the source and destination pixels get multiplied with their blend factors. Then, the blend operation takes the two products and calculates the result that will get stored in the render target. Expressed in pseudocode, it would look like this: ```c dstRGB = colorOperation(srcRGB * srcColorFactor, dstRGB * dstColorFactor); dstA = alphaOperation(srcA * srcAlphaFactor, dstA * dstAlphaFactor); ``` Where the functions `colorOperation(src, dst)` and `alphaOperation(src, dst)` can return one of the following: - `src + dst` - `src - dst` - `dst - src` - `min(src, dst)` - `max(src, dst)` The red, green, and blue components are always multiplied with the first, second, and third components of the SDL_BlendFactor, respectively. The fourth component is not used. The alpha component is always multiplied with the fourth component of the SDL_BlendFactor. The other components are not used in the alpha calculation. Support for these blend modes varies for each renderer. To check if a specific SDL_BlendMode is supported, create a renderer and pass it to either SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode or SDL_SetTextureBlendMode. They will return with an error if the blend mode is not supported. This list describes the support of custom blend modes for each renderer. All renderers support the four blend modes listed in the SDL_BlendMode enumeration. - **direct3d**: Supports all operations with all factors. However, some factors produce unexpected results with `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_MINIMUM` and `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_MAXIMUM`. - **direct3d11**: Same as Direct3D 9. - **opengl**: Supports the `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_ADD` operation with all factors. OpenGL versions 1.1, 1.2, and 1.3 do not work correctly here. - **opengles2**: Supports the `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_ADD`, `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_SUBTRACT`, `SDL_BLENDOPERATION_REV_SUBTRACT` operations with all factors. - **psp**: No custom blend mode support. - **software**: No custom blend mode support. Some renderers do not provide an alpha component for the default render target. The `SDL_BLENDFACTOR_DST_ALPHA` and `SDL_BLENDFACTOR_ONE_MINUS_DST_ALPHA` factors do not have an effect in this case. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawBlendMode @sa SDL_SetTextureBlendMode @sa SDL_GetTextureBlendMode<br>返回：an SDL_BlendMode that represents the chosen factors and operations.<br>`srcColorFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the red, green, and blue components of the source pixels.<br>`dstColorFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the red, green, and blue components of the destination pixels.<br>`colorOperation`：the SDL_BlendOperation used to combine the red, green, and blue components of the source and destination pixels.<br>`srcAlphaFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the alpha component of the source pixels.<br>`dstAlphaFactor`：the SDL_BlendFactor applied to the alpha component of the destination pixels.<br>`alphaOperation`：the SDL_BlendOperation used to combine the alpha component of the source and destination pixels. |
| <a id="api-6f278b0b23aaf5ad"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.CopyProperties(uint src, uint dst)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11962) | Copy a group of properties. Copy all the properties from one group of properties to another, with the exception of properties requiring cleanup (set using SDL_SetPointerPropertyWithCleanup()), which will not be copied. Any property that already exists on `dst` will be overwritten. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. This function acquires simultaneous mutex locks on both the source and destination property sets. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`src`：the properties to copy.<br>`dst`：the destination properties. |

The definition of copysign is that ``copysign(x, y) = abs(x) * sign(y)``. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, ``-INF < = y < = f`` Range: `-INF < = z < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_copysignf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_copysignf @sa SDL_fabs<br>返回：the floating point value with the sign of y and the magnitude of x.<br>`x`：floating point value to use as the magnitude.<br>`y`：floating point value to use as the sign. |

The floor of `x` is the largest integer `y` such that `y < = x`, i.e `x` rounded down to the nearest integer. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_floor for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the floor of `x`.<br>`x`：floating point value. |
| <a id="api-4b7216a660ea73c0"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.FlushEvent(uint type)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16615) | Clear events of a specific type from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that match `type`. If you need to remove a range of event types, use SDL_FlushEvents() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. If you have user events with custom data that needs to be freed, you should use SDL_PeepEvents() to remove and clean up those events before calling this function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvents<br>`type`：the type of event to be cleared; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-25cb526399fe8873"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.FlushEvents(uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16078) | Clear events of a range of types from the event queue. This will unconditionally remove any events from the queue that are in the range of `minType` to `maxType`, inclusive. If you need to remove a single event type, use SDL_FlushEvent() instead. It's also normal to just ignore events you don't care about in your event loop without calling this function. This function only affects currently queued events. If you want to make sure that all pending OS events are flushed, you can call SDL_PumpEvents() on the main thread immediately before the flush call. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FlushEvent<br>`minType`：the low end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`maxType`：the high end of event type to be cleared, inclusive; see SDL_EventType for details. |
| <a id="api-594499939c6a229a"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Fmod(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14248) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmodf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmodf @sa SDL_modf @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`.<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0. |
| <a id="api-906c36f803ad7866"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Fmodf(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12885) | Return the floating-point remainder of `x / y` Divides `x` by `y`, and returns the remainder. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF`, `y != 0` Range: `-y < = z < = y` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_fmod for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_fmod @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_modff @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>返回：the remainder of `x / y`.<br>`x`：the numerator.<br>`y`：the denominator. Must not be 0. |
| <a id="api-b44f22a4076cdd51"></a>[`public static Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.FromProvider(BGCS.Runtime.INativeModuleProvider provider, System.Func<BGCS.Runtime.NativeModuleSession, BGCS.Runtime.NativeAllocator>? temporaryAllocatorFactory = null)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11397) | 当前生成的 ABI 声明；地址、缓冲区及回调的有效期由所属原生 owner 保证。 |
| <a id="api-c62b4bf9bd992c3f"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GamepadEventsEnabled()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12265) | Query the state of gamepad event processing. If gamepad events are disabled, you must call SDL_UpdateGamepads() yourself and check the state of the gamepad when you want gamepad information. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetGamepadEventsEnabled<br>返回：true if gamepad events are being processed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-266ab7c602f2a63a"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetAudioDeviceGain(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12174) | Get the gain of an audio device. The gain of a device is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio devices default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). Physical devices may not have their gain changed, only logical devices, and this function will always return -1.0f when used on physical devices. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioDeviceGain<br>返回：the gain of the device or -1.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`devid`：the audio device to query. |
| <a id="api-cbae4a0c2749a2cb"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCameraPosition Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetCameraPosition(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12195) | Get the position of the camera in relation to the system. Most platforms will report UNKNOWN, but mobile devices, like phones, can often make a distinction between cameras on the front of the device (that points towards the user, for taking "selfies") and cameras on the back (for filming in the direction the user is facing). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCameras<br>返回：the position of the camera on the system hardware.<br>`instance_id`：the camera device instance ID. |
| <a id="api-0618ac1431a7eecb"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetCpuCacheLineSize()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16677) | Determine the L1 cache line size of the CPU. This is useful for determining multi-threaded structure padding or SIMD prefetch sizes. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the L1 cache line size of the CPU, in bytes. |
| <a id="api-ad1c1c9537f58112"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayOrientation Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetCurrentDisplayOrientation(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13837) | Get the orientation of a display. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the SDL_DisplayOrientation enum value of the display, or `SDL_ORIENTATION_UNKNOWN` if it isn't available.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-bfdf3feb63494c5e"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetCurrentThreadId()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14984) | Get the thread identifier for the current thread. This thread identifier is as reported by the underlying operating system. If SDL is running on a platform that does not support threads the return value will always be zero. This function also returns a valid thread ID when called from the main thread. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetThreadID<br>返回：the ID of the current thread. |
| <a id="api-4c106da05e4a902a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDayOfWeek(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12660) | Get the day of week for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a value between 0 and 6 (0 being Sunday) if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date. |
| <a id="api-afba4b3337a63065"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDayOfYear(int year, int month, int day)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15105) | Get the day of year for a calendar date. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the day of year [0-365] if the date is valid or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year component of the date.<br>`month`：the month component of the date.<br>`day`：the day component of the date. |
| <a id="api-ac36b2182750741c"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDaysInMonth(int year, int month)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11683) | Get the number of days in a month for a given year. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the number of days in the requested month or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`year`：the year.<br>`month`：the month [1-12]. |
| <a id="api-6b8e4aaf52d04a55"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDirect3D9AdapterIndex(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15180) | Get the D3D9 adapter index that matches the specified display. The returned adapter index can be passed to `IDirect3D9::CreateDevice` and controls on which monitor a full screen application will appear. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：the D3D9 adapter index on success or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-380f6d9a1d330339"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDisplayContentScale(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13199) | Get the content scale of a display. The content scale is the expected scale for content based on the DPI settings of the display. For example, a 4K display might have a 2.0 (200%) display scale, which means that the user expects UI elements to be twice as big on this display, to aid in readability. After window creation, SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale() should be used to query the content scale factor for individual windows instead of querying the display for a window and calling this function, as the per-window content scale factor may differ from the base value of the display it is on, particularly on high-DPI and/or multi-monitor desktop configurations. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowDisplayScale @sa SDL_GetDisplays<br>返回：the content scale of the display, or 0.0f on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-e4fdd12bc2eeff49"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetDisplayProperties(uint displayID)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13609) | Get the properties associated with a display. The following read-only properties are provided by SDL: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_HDR_ENABLED_BOOLEAN`: true if the display has HDR headroom above the SDR white point. This is for informational and diagnostic purposes only, as not all platforms provide this information at the display level. On KMS/DRM: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_KMSDRM_PANEL_ORIENTATION_NUMBER`: the "panel orientation" property for the display in degrees of clockwise rotation. Note that this is provided only as a hint, and the application is responsible for any coordinate transformations needed to conform to the requested display orientation. On Wayland: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WAYLAND_WL_OUTPUT_POINTER`: the wl_output associated with the display On Windows: - `SDL_PROP_DISPLAY_WINDOWS_HMONITOR_POINTER`: the monitor handle (HMONITOR) associated with the display @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：a valid property ID on success or 0 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information.<br>`displayID`：the instance ID of the display to query. |
| <a id="api-6df8d7c593617aa4"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButtonLabel Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadButtonLabelForType(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadType type, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepadButton button)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13692) | Get the label of a button on a gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadButtonLabel<br>返回：the SDL_GamepadButtonLabel enum corresponding to the button label.<br>`type`：the type of gamepad to check.<br>`button`：a button index (one of the SDL_GamepadButton values). |
| <a id="api-9d46f722c0245845"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadGuidForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11537) | Get the implementation-dependent GUID of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GUIDToString @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>返回：the GUID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns a zero GUID.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID. |
| <a id="api-0d54ea7bd37fc9c8"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadPlayerIndexForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13762) | Get the player index of a gamepad. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPlayerIndex @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>返回：the player index of a gamepad, or -1 if it's not available.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID. |
| <a id="api-02f993a45d50aa01"></a>[`public ushort Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.GetGamepadProductForId(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11859) | Get the USB product ID of a gamepad, if available. This can be called before any gamepads are opened. If the product ID isn't available this function returns 0. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadProduct @sa SDL_GetGamepads<br>返回：the USB product ID of the selected gamepad. If called on an invalid index, this function returns zero.<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID. |
